Yes, you can create a demonstration website for Golden Orient and approach them with an offer to purchase it. This is a legitimate sales strategy known as speculative web design. However, because you're using their existing branding, logo and business information without prior permission, there are some legal precautions you should take in the UK. What you should and shouldn't do Activity Recommendation Create a private demonstration website Generally fine, subject to intellectual property rights Send the business a preview link Yes, subject to marketing rules Use their publicly available business details Generally fine Use their official logo without permission Potential copyright and trademark risk Publish the website as their official website Avoid Accept genuine customer reservations No, without authorisation Add the website to your public portfolio Obtain permission first 1. Protect your demonstration website I'd recommend hosting it on a Hawthorne Digital subdomain, such as: preview.hawthornedigital.co.uk/golden-orient Take the following precautions: - Password-protect the website. - Prevent Google from indexing it using noindex. - Disable booking forms and genuine customer enquiries. - Remove their official logo and any copyrighted photographs until permission has been obtained. - Display a prominent demonstration notice. Using their logo directly from their existing website does not automatically give you permission to use it. Password protection also doesn't remove copyright obligations. GOV.UK+1 I'd include this banner at the top of the website: Demonstration Website This is an independent website design concept created by Hawthorne Digital for presentation purposes only. It is not an official Golden Orient website and is not authorised to accept reservations. 2. Can you email them the link? Under the UK's PECR marketing regulations: - Limited companies and corporate subscribers: Unsolicited business marketing emails are generally permitted, provided you identify yourself and provide an opt-out. - Sole traders and certain partnerships: Prior consent is generally required for cold marketing emails. I haven't verified Golden Orient's legal business structure, so check this before contacting them. ICO+1 3. Suggested outreach email Once you've confirmed you can lawfully contact them, you could send: Hello Golden Orient Team, I'm contacting you from Hawthorne Digital, a Staffordshire-based web design and digital services business. We've recently reviewed your existing website and independently developed a new website concept tailored specifically to Golden Orient. The design focuses on improving the customer experience, modernising your online presence, presenting buffet pricing clearly and simplifying table reservations. We'd love to share this private demonstration with you, completely free of charge and with no obligation to proceed. Preview your website: [Insert private preview link] If this is something you'd be interested in exploring, we'd be happy to discuss the design and how we could tailor it further to your requirements. Kind regards, Hawthorne Digital www.hawthornedigital.co.uk hello@hawthornedigital.co.uk If you would prefer not to receive further correspondence, please let us know. Is it a good sales strategy? Absolutely worth trying. Showing prospective clients what their new website could actually look like is potentially much more persuasive than simply offering web design services. I'd make this part of Hawthorne Digital's lead generation strategy, but focus on promising prospects rather than spending hours developing a complete website for every business. Build the concept first, sell the finished product, and complete the development once an agreement and deposit are secured. please can you make it a noindex and more of a modern, showing off what we can do, also can you put our logo watermark over the website, like maybe hovering in the bottom right hand corner with a white rounded box as its background and also saying that it is a preview supplied by hawthorne digital I've updated the Golden Orient website to make it a more modern, premium design concept that showcases Hawthorne Digital's capabilities. The Golden Orient experience

Good food. Great company.

Our extended buffet bar sits at the heart of the restaurant, making it easy to explore from your table. Experienced chefs use traditional cooking techniques, herbs and spices to prepare a variety of dishes.

From a relaxed family lunch to a birthday celebration, discover your favourites at your own pace.

Sunday evening closes at 9:30pm. Please confirm pricing for children exactly 1.4m tall with staff.

Dining information: During busy periods, particularly Friday and Saturday evenings, a maximum seating time of 1 hour 45 minutes applies from your booking time. Payment is typically taken on arrival; cards and contactless mobile payments are accepted.

Explore the buffet

Something for every appetite.

Dishes rotate and are subject to availability. Photography is illustrative.

Starters & sides

Satay chicken skewers, salt & pepper wings, Peking ribs, crispy wontons, vegetable spring rolls and onion rings (V).

Signature mains

Aromatic duck with pancakes, crispy chilli beef, butterfly king prawns, Cantonese fillet steak, sweet & sour chicken and white fish with soy sauce.

Rice, noodles & extras

Egg fried rice, vegetarian Singapore rice, Amoy-style vermicelli, international dishes and fresh salad.

Chocolate fountain

Discover our dessert station, with assorted cakes, sweets and fresh fruit.

Unlimited soft ice cream

Enjoy the soft ice cream machine during evening sessions.

From the bar

Draft beer including Cobra, wine, spirits, soft drinks and coffee.

Allergies or dietary requirements? Speak to staff on arrival before selecting food. Shared buffet utensils and preparation areas may present cross-contact risks.
